# Online Store Concepts: 5,017 niches (part 2 of 4: Electronics Accessories → Medical & Mobility Aids)

[← back to index](README.md) · 1433 entries in this file · 154 categories in total

Each concept lists a target customer, business model, best-fit open-source store platform, price point and margin, where the product comes from, the marketing channel to start with and a startup-cost band (Low <$2k · Medium $2k-15k · High >$15k). Treat the platform as a starting suggestion.

| Key | Platform | Repo | ⭐ | License | Best for | Concepts |
|---|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|
